The single-$\tilde\Delta$ contraction with matching symmetries lives in the symbol calculus with the $\star$-contraction $\Psi^a\chi_o\to\hbar\tilde\Delta^{ao}$ (W10), which is not the product of a graded-commutative algebra: Theorem B′'s hypotheses are not met by it.

Consistency with the verified results: for $\ell=1$ there is no output pair, so (5′.12) on host (i) is faithful and has one $\tilde\Delta$ — W6/W6a/W7's $10^{-13}$ zeros are exactly the case the obstruction allows.

#### Multi-string test

Not run (brief: stop at the first failing leg; a verdict only on a host whose single-string control passes). Its machinery is in place (`residual_bins`, `C_n` reused unchanged); on host (iii) it would measure the residual of the bare-index-sum structure, which is not the object of D-a.

#### Verdict

\subsection*{Result}
**Verdict (C): host (iii) is not faithful, and no host of its kind can be.** It is faithful for slot symmetries (W8a: equal-parity output and input pairs both survive, all $\hat p_{k,\ell}$ even) but its single contraction is the bare index sum (5′.15), not A′'s $\tilde\Delta$-contraction; the single-string control fails at the free level ($\hat{\tilde Q}\Theta\ne0$, $\hat{\tilde Q}^2\ne0$; (5′.14)). The general obstruction above shows that faithful slot symmetries and a single-$\tilde\Delta$ contraction exclude each other on every graded-commutative polynomial host with an even parameter. Together with §5′.6 (hosts (i), (ii) inconsistent or dead for $\ell\ge2$; (iv) nilpotent parameter), the multi-string leading-order corollary of A′ **has no test as a Koszul-brace structure of differential operators on a single graded-commutative polynomial host in the strand variables with even $\hbar$** — not for lack of a construction but by the parity counting of the $\delta$-contraction. The question "does A′ give curved $L_\infty$ to leading order on multi-string inputs" is therefore, *within that class*, not (A) false and not (B) supported: it is *not posed* at the operator level. In particular the question as §5′.3 posed it — the doubled symbol algebra with its signs fixed, which is exactly host (iii) — is answered **negatively**; it is reposed for the symbol/$\star$-calculus of W10 (where "curved $L_\infty$" would have to be defined without Theorem B′) or with an odd parameter in a non-graded-commutative host (the MV question, §5′.6 remark). Scope: exact for the class $T=(A^{\otimes\ell}\otimes B^{\otimes k})\circ V$ with slot-uniform, parity-homogeneous raising maps $A,B$ and $\delta$-contraction (per-component dressings $s(o,a)$ are covered, since they do not change parities); **untested, not excluded**: hosts with *two* sets of generators ($\chi_o$ for outputs, $\psi^a$ for inputs), a non-polynomial product, the $\star$-calculus (W10), an odd parameter.

*What R32's open note now says (applied to the ledger 2026-09-19, calibrated per critic 026D Block 4):* "the multi-string leading-order corollary is proven on single strings only (Lemma B1); on multi-string inputs it has no formulation as a Koszul-brace structure of differential operators on a single graded-commutative polynomial host in the strand variables with even $\hbar$ (slot-uniform, parity-homogeneous raising maps on the slots, $\delta$-contraction) — within that class every such host projects a slot symmetry (host (i), 026 W7″) or replaces A′'s $\tilde\Delta$-contraction by a bare index sum (host (iii), 026 (5′.15)); untested, not excluded: hosts with separate input/output generators, a non-polynomial product, the $\star$-calculus (026 W10), an odd parameter in a non-graded-commutative host." *The paper's Theorem B:* (B′.4) with the single-string restriction of its $\hbar$-corollary, as before; the multi-string extension should be stated as open *and* as not an operator statement. *Correction to §5′.6 (not edited):* its "sanity of the free part" for (iii) dropped one $\tilde\Delta$; the free part fails there (W8b′), and the table's "expected yes" for (iii) is right for slot symmetries but the row lacks a column "contraction = A′'s?", which is "no" for (iii) and "yes, $\ell\le1$ only" for (i).

\subsection*{Verification}
Script `part_b_host3_checks` (driver run 29 s; full run in `main`): W8a PASS (65 monomial instances, 27 equal-parity output pairs, 12 equal-parity input pairs, parity of all terms $0$); W8b FINDING (control nonzero, (5′.14)); W8b′ PASS (diagnosis: $\tilde\Delta\tilde\Delta=\mathbb 1$ to $0$, operator $\hat{\tilde Q}^2\chi_c$ against the matrix $\tilde\Delta V_2V_2\tilde\Delta\chi_c$ to $10^{-16}$ by an independent path, $|V_2V_2|=1$, $|V_2\tilde\Delta V_2|=0$). Falsifiability: W8b′ fails with $\Delta$ in place of $\tilde\Delta$ or with the five-factor prediction of §5′.6. A first W8a version tested the $\chi$-monomial alone for evenness (check typo, FAIL) — corrected to the parity of the whole term before any interpretation. Not done: W8c, W8 (legs after the failing one); the two-generator-set host (outside the obstruction's class).

## Working notes (B′)

**WB1. For B′b — the corrected criterion and its symbol form (idea, not derived).** (5′.9) holds for any linear $D$ and even $a$, so the braces of $\hat{\mathfrak p}$ form a curved $L_\infty$ algebra iff $e^{-a}\hat{\mathfrak p}^2e^a=F(a)^2$ for all even $a$, $F=e^{-a}\hat{\mathfrak p}e^a$. In the symbol calculus (5.11), $F(a)$ is $\mathbb H$ with $\Psi\to\hbar\tilde\Delta\partial_\chi$ acting on $e^{a(\chi)}$ and normal-ordered against $e^{-a}$; $F^2$ is the product of two such factors with *no* contraction between them, i.e. the $s=0$ term of $\mathbb H\star\mathbb H$ evaluated on $e^a$ — so the expected corrected Theorem B is: **curved $L_\infty$ on $W$ ⇔ the $s\ge1$ part of $\hat{\mathfrak p}\star\hat{\mathfrak p}$ vanishes**, i.e. $\{\mathbb H,\mathbb H\}=0$ (A′) at $s=1$ and the higher-contraction terms ((5.17) at $s=2$) at $s\ge2$, with the $s=0$ term $\mathbb H\cdot\mathbb H\ne0$ no longer an obstruction. The free theory is consistent with this (P4e: $s\ge1$ terms $Q^2$, $Q\Theta$, $\mathrm{str}Q^2$ all vanish and the relations hold). To be proven with parities tracked; the Wick-type statement "$D_1D_2e^a-(e^{-a}D_1e^a)(e^{-a}D_2e^a)e^a=$ contracted terms" is the lemma. Second open point for B′b: the host space. $S_\varepsilon\mathcal H$ kills equal-parity output pairs (P3), $S_{\bar\varepsilon}\mathcal H$ mis-symmetrises the inputs; candidates are the doubled-variable symbol algebra (inputs $\Psi$ in $\varepsilon$, outputs $\chi$ in $\bar\varepsilon$; MS's odd-$x$ bookkeeping) or a restriction to the parity-homogeneous sub-family. Also to fix in B′b: (5.15)/W10's "$\mathbb H\cdot\mathbb H=0$", §5.4's $m_2(\Psi,\Psi)$ on $S_\varepsilon$ (zero, critic), §7 row B.

**WB2. For B′c — status of WB1 after B′b, and the host-space options (2026-09-14).** (a) WB1's first equivalence is proven (Theorem B′, (B′.4)); its second, "$\Leftrightarrow(\hat{\mathfrak p}\star\hat{\mathfrak p})_{s\ge1}=0$" with W10's symbol contraction number, is **false** (counterexample $\hat p_{2,0}$, W2; hand argument (W.2)): the $L_\infty$ residual also contains terms where derivatives of the left factor hit the $\partial a$ factors produced by the right factor, invisible in the symbol calculus and cancelling only for odd homogeneous $D$. (b) Open: whether those extra terms vanish for the actual $\Delta$-raised family at $s_{\rm W}=1$ on multi-string inputs (they carry $\hbar^{n-1}$, the same order as A′). If they do not, "A′ ⇒ curved $L_\infty$ to leading order" is false on $W$ beyond single strings, and the corrected Theorem B for the paper is (B′.4) with the $\hbar$-corollary restricted to single-string inputs (Lemma B1) — a weaker but exact statement. Test: build $\hat Q+\hbar\hat\ell_2$ with correct signs (see (c)) on the 12-strand toy in `GPoly`, $a$ of degree 2, extract the $\hbar^1$ coefficient of $\mathcal R(a)$. (c) Décalage: the operators $\hat p_{k,\ell}$ of (5.1) on $S_{\bar\varepsilon}\mathcal H$ must carry the suspension signs of $\mathcal H\to\mathcal H[1]$ for $[\hat Q,\hat\ell_2]=0$ to hold as a graded commutator; the record never wrote them (the old §5 was parity-blind, W11). Without them any interacting toy on $S_{\bar\varepsilon}$ tests the convention, not the theorem. (d) Host options: (i) $S_\varepsilon\mathcal H$ — inputs right, $\bar\varepsilon$-symmetric output pairs killed (P3); (ii) $S_{\bar\varepsilon}\mathcal H$ — outputs right, inputs mis-symmetrised, décalage needed; (iii) doubled symbol algebra — both symmetries, MS/CFL bookkeeping, A′'s own setting (7c); recommended in §5′.3. (e) §5.4's $m_2(\Psi,\Psi)$ on $S_\varepsilon$ is zero (critic) and must be restated on (iii); §7 row B to be rewritten from (B′.4).

**W12. Quantifier counterexample for Theorem B′ (critic 026C, recorded B′d).** $A=\Lambda(\theta)$, $D=\theta\partial_\theta$ (even, order 1, a derivation): $\ell^W_0=D(1)=0$, $\ell^W_1=D$, $\ell^W_{\ge2}=0$. $A_{\rm even}$ consists of constants $c$, on which $F_D(c)=F_{D^2}(c)=0$, so "$F_{D^2}(a)=F_D(a)^2$ for all even $a\in A$" holds vacuously; but the arity-one relation fails, $(\ell^W_1)^2\theta=D^2\theta=\theta\ne0$. With one auxiliary odd parameter $\eta$ and $a=\eta\theta\in(A\otimes\Lambda(\eta))_{\rm even}$: $F_D(a)=D(a)=a$, $F_{D^2}(a)=a$, $F_D(a)^2=0$, residual $a\ne0$ — the corrected criterion (B′.4) fails, as it must. Hence the quantifier in (B′.2), (B′.4), special case (i), §0 and the §7 row must range over even $a\in A\otimes\Lambda$ (equivalently, the multilinear relations in all input-parity sectors). Machine: W5/W5′. Credit: critic 026C, hunt 2.

**W13 (2026-09-14, from the proofreader of the companion note):** Eqs (1.4) and (3.11) state the one-leg raising identity in the unsigned (naive) form; the correct form, used from §5′ on, is (5′.2) with the sign $-(-1)^{\varepsilon_c}$. The equations are left as written for the record; every downstream use in §3 is through the $\tilde\Delta$-raised products, whose signs are fixed in §1.2, so no result changes.

## §6 Self-review

**B′c pointer (2026-09-14).** Items 1–4 below were written for the old Theorem B; where they say "Theorem B", "iff $\hat{\mathfrak p}^2=0$" or "exact only for a full (all-genus) IBL$_\infty$ family", read §5′ (Theorem B′, (B′.4); the all-genus reading is withdrawn pending WB2 (b)); the cyclicity redirect (items 1, 4(d)) is unaffected (§5′.4).

**1. Objective answered or only the literal task?** The literal task (part B statement with proof or obstruction, the $O(g_s^2)$ instance, literature status, ledger rows) is done. The motivating objective — a general $L_\infty\Leftrightarrow\mathrm{IBL}_\infty$ correspondence usable for multi-string actions — is answered in a form that departs from the literal request but is exact: *the correspondence is Theorem A′ ($\mathcal H$ side) together with Theorem B ($W$ side), and on $W$ the canonical $L_\infty$ structure built from the $p_{k,\ell}$ is the brace structure, which (a) is exact only for a full (all-genus) IBL$_\infty$ family, (b) is not cyclic, so it does not by itself give an action on $W$.* The goal of more general actions — cyclic $L_\infty$ on $W$ from the $p_{k,\ell}$ — is shown to require leaving the brace family (HS, Route C are the known ways). That is a redirect, flagged, not a completion of the letter of the task.

**2. Weakest step and what shored it up.** (5.16), the identification "brace arity $n$ ⇔ $\hbar^{n+s-2}$ ⇔ contraction number $s$", is a counting argument (W10) checked against CFL's own $\hbar^{k+g-1}$ weights but not machine-tested in operator form (W11). It is shored up by: the brace theorem (5.13)–(5.14), machine-checked in its combinatorial content (B1d–f); 7c's generating-function verification of the $s=1$ relation; and the explicit $s=2$ term (5.17), computed and nonzero (B3). Second-weakest: the non-cyclicity obstruction (5.27)–(5.28) is a hand computation of one component; its structural version (5.29) is elementary.

**3. Not checked, and why acceptable.** (i) Operator form of $\hat{\mathfrak p}$ on bare physical polynomials (W11): the statements that depend on it are A′ (already verified by 7c through the generating function, which is the *definition* of the operator by CFL Prop. 7.2) and (5.16) (counting). (ii) Koszul signs in Lemma B1 on odd inputs: the inclusion–exclusion argument (5.6) is sign-transparent (a common Koszul factor per $J$), and the physical fields are even. (iii) The $O(\hbar^{\ge3})$ terms of (5.15): not needed for any claim. (iv) Whether $\hat{\mathfrak p}^2\ne0$ persists after adding the genus-one products of a quantum action: outside the record (§3.6).

**4. What a stronger physicist would find in five minutes.** (a) That the brace structure is the well-known "BV$_\infty$ / higher antibracket" structure and that its MC equation is the IBL MC equation (§5.2 (5.9)) — found, and cited as implicit in MS. (b) That the non-augmented family is the Taylor expansion of $S$ about a background (3.33), so Theorem A′ "has no content beyond $(S,S)=0$" — stated in §3.4; the content of Task 7 is then the *negative* result (Statement A false, R16 wrong) plus the precise multi-string statement (Theorem B). (c) That the cotangent lift (5.30) is R13's mechanism — noted. (d) Possibly: a *different* symmetric-algebra product on $W$ (e.g. a shuffle/deconcatenation-type coproduct on the tensor coalgebra rather than the symmetric algebra) for which a Frobenius pairing exists, giving a cyclic brace-type structure. Not explored; recorded as the one open angle for a cyclic structure on $W$ itself.

**Depth checklist.** *Limiting cases:* free theory (5.17) $\to0$ (B3); single-string inputs (5.7); $\hbar\to1$ (5.28). *Second method:* the brace theorem re-derived (5.12)–(5.13) and machine-checked; A′ enters only through 7c (no operator-form second method, W11). *Regime of validity:* Theorem B holds for any family of maps $E_k\to E_\ell$ (not only $\Delta$-raised); the $\hbar$-filtration statement needs the family finite in $\ell$ at fixed input (CFL (7.5)) or formal completion; finite-dimensional $\mathcal H$ throughout, as in R1–R15. *Interpretation:* below.

**Interpretation for the paper and for the notes.** Repackaging the cyclic $L_\infty$ data of $\mathcal H$ as multi-output products does not create an $L_\infty$ theory with independent multi-string fields: the canonical structure on $W$ (Theorem B) is the coderivation $\hat{\mathfrak p}$ read through the algebra structure of $S\mathcal H$, its differential contains $\hat Q$ on every $\Psi_n$, it does not truncate, and it has no cyclic pairing. The HS theory is a *selection* of components of that structure (the $V_4$-slice (5.25)) re-weighted so that an action exists — which is why the HS relations are the $\min(k,\ell)=1$ ones (composite gauge law) and why $(2,2,0)$ never had to hold. Multi-string actions therefore come from choosing which braces to keep and finding a pairing for the choice (HS at each order, R26; Route C with multipliers, R13), not from an IBL$_\infty$ theorem; the IBL$_\infty$ relations fix the *products* (A′), the action is extra data.

**Sub-structure reading (orchestrator's question, 2026-09-08; W12).** The intended claim is an IBL$_\infty$ *sub-structure* with a chosen subset of components nonzero, as $A_2\subset A_\infty$ for Witten's OSFT or MS's "quantum $L_\infty$ + $\Delta$". The count: the smallest augmented sub-family containing $m_2$ is **not** $\{Q,\ell_2,\ell_3,p_{1,2},m_2\}$ — at order $g^2$ the relation $(1,3,0)$, $[\hat Q,\hat p_{1,3}]+\hat p_{1,2}\circ_1\hat p_{1,2}=0$, has the nonzero term $\hat p_{1,2}\circ_1\hat p_{1,2}$ (it equals $-[\hat Q,\hat p_{1,3}]\ne0$ by §3.1), so $p_{1,3}=\tilde\Delta^{\otimes3}V_4$ is forced exactly as $\ell_3$ is forced by $(3,1,0)$ and $p_{1,2}$ by $(2,2,0)$. The minimal candidate is therefore *all augmented components of $V_{\le4}$*, $F_4=\{Q,\ell_2,\ell_3,p_{1,2},m_2,p_{1,3}\}$, whose relations at $k+\ell\le4$ are $(1,1),(2,1),(3,1),(1,2),(1,3)$ — all hold (§3.1) — and $(2,2)$, which **fails by $\tfrac12M_{2,2}\ne0$** (§3.2–§3.3): the relation involves only $V_2,V_3,V_4$ components, all present in $F_4$, so the sub-algebra reading changes nothing about it. Relations with $k+\ell\ge5$ ($(4,1),(3,2),(2,3)$ at $g^3$, $(3,3)$ at $g^4$) fail for $F_4$ by terms that need $V_{\ge5}$ components, in the same way as the quartic-truncated $L_\infty$ fails at $(4,1)$: closure is "to the order kept", never exact for SFT (unlike $A_2\subset A_\infty$, which is exact because $m_2$ is associative; MS's "$L_\infty+\Delta$" is exact because the bivector is $Q$-invariant). In the non-augmented sense (MS-like, pairing included: add $p_{2,0},p_{0,2},p_{3,0},p_{0,3},p_{4,0},p_{0,4}$) the sub-family of all components of $V_{\le4}$ closes *exactly* under every relation with $k+\ell\le4$, including $(2,2,0)$ — that is Theorem A′ truncated by vertex rank, and it is consistent because the $(k,\ell)$ relation only involves $V_n$ with $n\le k+\ell$. **Consequence for §3.3's verdict:** under the sub-algebra reading R16's $(2,2,0)$ claim is still wrong in the augmented (CFL Def. 2.3) sense, and correct only as the non-augmented relation $B_{2,2}=2(A_1+A_2+A_3)+A_4=0$ of §3.3 — the wording proposed there stands; the paper can state "the components of $V_{\le4}$ satisfy the genus-zero relations in generating-function form to the order kept (non-augmented sense)", which is R16's statement corrected on both counts (augmentation, and $p_{1,3}$ included).

## §7 Proposed ledger rows and actions

One line each; the ledger is not edited by this record.

- **New row (Theorem A′), status Proven (§3.4) / Verified (7c table, D0, E1–E7).** "For graded-symmetric $V_n$ on $(\mathcal H,\omega_1)$ the non-augmented $\tilde\Delta$-raised family $\{p_{k,\ell}\}_{k,\ell\ge0}$ ($p_{k,0}=V_k$, $p_{0,\ell}=\tilde\Delta^{\otimes\ell}V_\ell$, $p_{k,1}=\ell_k$) satisfies the genus-zero relations (026 (3.29)) at every $(k,\ell)$ iff $(\mathcal H,\omega_1,\{\ell_n\})$ is cyclic $L_\infty$ — satisfies the genus-zero IBL relations in generating-function form (polarised master equation), of the non-augmented type CFL admit in §8; the degree bookkeeping requires $d=\tfrac12$, outside CFL's integer-$d$ hypotheses, and the slot symmetries need the per-slot dictionary of §1.2 (iii); equal to $\{S(\Psi+\chi),S(\Psi+\chi)\}=0$; the augmented (Def. 2.3) relations hold only at $\min(k,\ell)=1$; false in general (counterexample at $(2,2)$), at every $(k,\ell)\ge(2,2)$ equivalent to the extra condition $P_{k,\ell}=0$, seen to fail at $(2,2),(2,3),(3,2)$."
- **Theorem B′ (026 §5′.3), status Proven (§5′.3, quantifier corrected B′d) / Verified: identity (B′.1) W1–W4, converse-on-odd-sectors W5 (`codes/verify-linfty-ibl-repackaging.py` 99/99; the iff itself is proven, not machine-checked).** "Let $D=\hat{\mathfrak p}=\sum_{k,\ell}\hbar^{k-1}\hat p_{k,\ell}$ be a finite-order differential operator on a graded-commutative algebra $A$ (components of any parity; $\hat{\mathfrak p}$ is parity-inhomogeneous in every grading, 026 §5′.1). Its Koszul braces $\ell^W_n=\Phi^n_D$ form a curved $L_\infty$ algebra on $A$ (multilinear relations in all input-parity sectors) iff $F_{D^2}(a)=F_D(a)^2$ for all even $a\in A\otimes\Lambda$, $\Lambda$ any auxiliary Grassmann algebra, $F_D(a):=e^{-a}De^a$ (over even $a\in A$ alone only the even-input relations follow, 026 W12) — equivalently iff the braces of $D^2$ are the shuffle square of the braces of $D$ (026 (B′.2)), equivalently iff all terms of $e^{-a}D^2e^a$ with at least one derivative of the left factor acting on the output $F_D(a)$ vanish (Lemma W, (W.7)). For odd homogeneous $D$ this is $D^2=0$ on $A$, with the same quantifier (the classical brace theorem, BDA/Akman/Voronov, now with its hypothesis); for even or inhomogeneous $D$ it is not: the free theory $\hat{\tilde Q}+\Theta/\hbar$ has curved-$L_\infty$ braces with $D^2\ne0$ (026 §5′.2), and $D=\hat p_{2,0}$ has $(D\star D)_{s\ge1}=0$ with non-$L_\infty$ braces (026 W2). Refuted: the earlier Theorem B 'curved $L_\infty$ iff $\hat{\mathfrak p}^2=0$' and the symbol-form criterion '$(\hat{\mathfrak p}\star\hat{\mathfrak p})_{s\ge1}=0$'. On single-string inputs the braces are $\ell^W_n(\Psi_1..\Psi_n)=\hbar^{n-1}\sum_\ell p_{n,\ell}$ (Lemma B1) and Theorem A′ is the leading-$\hbar$ content of the criterion there. Host: theorem host-independent. For $\ell=1$ the naive coderivation extension on $S_\varepsilon\mathcal H$ (026 (5′.12), no décalage sign) is verified to arity 3; for $\ell\ge2$ no single graded-commutative polynomial host in the strand variables with even $\hbar$ realises the family with A′'s contraction, in the class with slot-uniform parity-homogeneous raising maps on the slots and $\delta$-contraction (026 §5′.7; other hosts — separate input/output generators, non-polynomial product, $\star$-calculus, odd parameter — untested, not excluded); the doubled symbol algebra is bookkeeping only ($S_\varepsilon\mathcal H$ kills the $\bar\varepsilon$-symmetric output pairs of $m_2,\hat p_{1,2},\hat p_{0,2}$ on physical inputs, 026 P3)."
- **Open note (not a row; note under Theorem B′), Verdict (C) in calibrated form:** "the multi-string leading-order corollary is proven on single strings only (Lemma B1); on multi-string inputs it has no formulation as a Koszul-brace structure of differential operators on a single graded-commutative polynomial host in the strand variables with even $\hbar$ (slot-uniform, parity-homogeneous raising maps on the slots, $\delta$-contraction) — within that class every such host projects a slot symmetry (host (i), 026 W7″) or replaces A′'s $\tilde\Delta$-contraction by a bare index sum (host (iii), 026 (5′.15)); untested, not excluded: hosts with separate input/output generators, a non-polynomial product, the $\star$-calculus (026 W10), an odd parameter in a non-graded-commutative host." The earlier "first obstruction is the one-loop term (5.17)" is withdrawn.
